| QB-01 | Establish clean solution boundaries | **DONE** | Web host `net10.0` and independent of the desktop project; WASM client off Windows TFM. |

| QB-05 | Add typed, authenticated API to existing web host | **DONE (first slice)** | `/api/v1` auth/customers/health, ProblemDetails + codes, correlation ids, OpenAPI, typed clients. |

customers + products done** | Customers and products/variants are exposed via `/api/v1` on PostgreSQL; suppliers/units/categories/brands/groups are modeled in the schema but not all exposed as dedicated endpoints yet (tax rates + discounts land with QB-12). |
| QB-07 | Replace journals with balanced, immutable ledger | **DONE (verified live on PostgreSQL 18.6)** | Chart of accounts, periods, atomic posting, reversals, source idempotency, derived trial balance. |
| QB-08 | Transactional authoritative inventory | **DONE (verified live on PostgreSQL 18.6)** | Movement+balance atomicity, no oversell under concurrency, replay-safe references, reconciliation report. |
| QB-09 | Atomic idempotent server-side sale posting | **DONE (verified live on PostgreSQL 18.6)** | `POST/GET /api/v1/pos/sales`; one transaction = sale + snapshots + stock + payment + balanced journal + audit; `Idempotency-Key` replay returns the stored sale, changed payload refused; race-safe against oversell. |
| QB-10 | Cash register, safe payments and receipts | **DONE (verified live on PostgreSQL 18.6)** | Drawer sessions (one open per register), derived expected cash, counted/variance close with audited movements and reasons; card disabled (`PAYMENT_METHOD_DISABLED`); snapshot receipts (JSON + printable HTML with a provisional template); full refunds (balanced reversal + stock return + drawer movement, replay-safe). |
| QB-11 | Immutable invoice and credit workflow | **DONE (verified live on PostgreSQL 18.6)** | Draft/issue/void/credit lifecycle with server-computed totals; `INV-` numbers allocated transactionally (row-locked sequence + partial unique index); snapshot lines survive catalog renames; posted terms immutable (`409 INVOICE_NOT_DRAFT`); payment allocation with balance enforcement and `Idempotency-Key` receipts; full credit note references the original and reverses its entry; printable HTML document. |
| QB-12 | Purchasing, expense, tax and discount integrity | **DONE (verified live on PostgreSQL 18.6)** | PO vs receipt separation (a PO alone never moves stock, `PO-`/`GRN-`/`PR-` numbers, snapshot lines); replay-safe partial receipts and returns validated under a row lock; expense posting with exactly one balanced entry and append-only reversal (`ALREADY_REVERSED`); tax rates as bounded exact decimals with a single default; discount definitions validated before storage; PO-line draft guard narrowed for receipt bookkeeping. |
| QB-13 | Financial and operational reports from authoritative facts | **DONE (verified live on PostgreSQL 18.6)** | Balance sheet, P&L, cash, stock and sales statements derived per request from posted journal lines and inventory movements; no mutable balance CRUD; business-scoped responses with base-currency-only filters and provisional money excluded; 400/401/404 negative controls; tie-outs against ledger, inventory balances and the balance-sheet identity. |
| QB-14 | Security, structured audit and real backup/restore | **DONE (verified live on PostgreSQL 18.6)** | Typed ProblemDetails failures (400/401/403/500 with stable codes, `traceId`, no stack/SQL leakage); no database-writing error logger; redaction before logging/persistence; masked, correlated authentication audit in append-only `audit_events` plus a scoped `audit.view` feed; Data Protection key-ring path; real `pg_dump` backup/restore scripts with a disposable-database restore drill (18/18 reconciled); DML-only PostgreSQL application role (DDL refused); static secret scan clean. |
| QB-15 | Remove web client database dependencies | **DONE (current surface)** | Browser bundle contains no Npgsql/EF/data-access assets; CI bundle check still TODO (QB-26). |
| QB-16 | Extract shared MudBlazor Razor components | **DONE (web + desktop)** | `QtsBilling.Ui` RCL holds the customer page/dialog/layout/nav with feature-interface injection only; the page renders in the web host and in the MAUI desktop host, and an HTML-render smoke proves the same component works outside a host with stub services. |
| QB-17 | Implement device-local SQLite persistence | **DONE (library + tests)** | `QtsBilling.Persistence.Sqlite` holds the specification 4.2 schema (cache vs. durable pending tables in one WAL file), checked scaled-integer money/quantity converters, a durable outbox with idempotent replay, database-enforced constraints, startup integrity/compatibility checks with a pre-migration snapshot and its own migration history; 20 temp-file SQLite tests green. The MAUI host consumes it (QB-18). |
| QB-18 | Create MAUI Windows Blazor Hybrid application | **DONE (Windows host)** | `QtsBilling.Maui` (net10.0-windows10.0.19041.0) hosts the shared `QtsBilling.Ui` components in a WebView2, opens the QB-17 SQLite store, keeps the session in the DPAPI secure store and starts offline; the shared customer page renders 6 live rows on the desktop and in the browser after the missing-`StateHasChanged` defect was fixed. MSIX packaging stays with QB-27. |
| QB-19 | Implement offline cash-only sale capture | **DONE (desktop capture)** | The shared point-of-sale view captures cash sales into the device-local store atomically (draft + lines + cash + outbox), shows a clearly provisional receipt with no invoice number, and lists the pending queue; a capture is accepted only after it is durably stored, one operation id can never create two sales, and the offline permission is limited to `sales.post` on an enrolled location/register. Verified with the server stopped and after a restart. |
| QB-20 | Server idempotent sync-push handler | **DONE (endpoint + transaction)** | `POST /api/v1/sync/operations` (and a batch) accepts an offline operation exactly once: the receipt row and the sale share one transaction, a replay returns the original outcome, the same key with a different body is a stable 409, an unenrolled device or another business is refused, and the sale is posted by the shared QB-09 path (extracted into `SalePosting`). Verified live with five pushes of one operation. |
| QB-21 | Implement safe server master-data change feed | **DONE (feed + revisions)** | Every master-data write appends its projection in the same transaction, with a row-locked per-business revision, so the feed order is the commit order; a device walks the `(revision, ordinal)` cursor, a deactivation is a tombstone, the feed is permission scoped and a cursor past the server is answered with `RESYNC_REQUIRED`. Verified live with four consecutive commit-ordered revisions. |
| QB-22 | MAUI push/pull coordinator and conflict-resolution queue | **DONE (device loop + conflict queue)** | The desktop client pushes its local outbox to POST /api/v1/sync/operations, stores the receipt, pulls the change feed into its cache and keeps a conflict queue for the operator when a server change touches a pending local sale. Verified live: an offline capture was pushed and posted exactly once, the feed was pulled and the cursor advanced, and the sync card shows the queue, the conflicts and the revision. |
| QB-23 | Historical data export/inventory tooling | **DONE (snapshot inventory)** | QtsBilling.Migration inventory classifies an exported, hash-verified snapshot (counts, field types, duplicate source ids, credential fields). MongoDB is no longer a dependency of the repository: the legacy services are deleted and nothing in the solution talks to a legacy database. |
| QB-24 | Deterministic transform and PostgreSQL import | **DONE (deterministic + idempotent)** | The mapping is data (docs/migration/mapping.sample.json), the target id is derived from the source identity, the load is an idempotent upsert with the append-only identifier map, and a record that cannot be mapped is quarantined with its source id. A rerun updates the same rows and creates no duplicate. |
| QB-25 | Produce signed reconciliation report and account-reset plan | **DONE (signed report)** | The report compares the source, transformed and imported counts per collection, lists every exception with its source id, and carries a SHA-256 plus an HMAC signature over the canonical body (the key comes from the environment; without it the report says unsigned). A legacy value without a counterpart blocks the go-live until it is decided. |
| QB-26 | Focused tests and CI pipelines | **DONE (pipelines green)** | .github/workflows/ci.yml builds the server, the WASM client and the libraries on Linux and the desktop client on Windows, runs the domain/contract suite and the device-store suite (no external services) and the secret scan; 
elease.yml publishes the server and the desktop client from a version tag. |
| QB-27 | Operational runbooks, backup, packaging and observability | **DONE (runbooks + packaging + observability)** | docs/migration/deployment.md now carries the deployment, backup/restore, credential rotation, device, failure, packaging and observability procedures; the backup/restore tooling is scripted and rehearsed. |
| QB-28 | End-to-end rehearsal and pilot approval | **DONE (rehearsal passes; the pilot approval is a business decision)** | scripts/qb28-rehearsal.ps1 runs build, tests, secret scan, health check, the idempotent push smoke, the change-feed smoke, a verified backup and a restore drill: 7 steps, 0 failed. The pilot itself (business, devices, cashiers, window) still needs the business owner. |
| QB-29 | Retire the legacy desktop and data-access projects | **DONE** | The legacy desktop and data-access projects were removed from the solution; the MAUI client (QB-18) is the sole desktop host. |
| QB-30 | Production cutover and post-cutover integrity verification | **DONE (cutover script; the production run needs the real environment and the owner's codeword)** | scripts/qb30-cutover.ps1 gates the cutover behind the business owner's codeword, a verified backup, the idempotent frozen import, a signed reconciliation whose exceptions must be approved, the identity reset, the device enrolment and the ledger/stock/tenant/numbering/payment integrity checks. Rehearsed here: 18 gates, 0 failed, with the stop/fix-forward plan recorded. |
| QB-31 | Shared commercial amount contract and the inclusive-tax posting fix | **DONE (unit-tested and verified on PostgreSQL)** | One pure calculation for the server, the browser and the device; the journal credits the revenue base; the tax composition of a posted line is an immutable snapshot. The critical finding F11 (an unbalanced journal on a tax-inclusive line) is closed. |
| QB-32 | A safe PostgreSQL/API regression harness and the native readiness ledger | **DONE (the four safety cases pass against the real PostgreSQL)** | The fixture creates its own random qts_test_ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¦ database on an allowlisted host, applies the real migrations and runs the real host; a production-shaped target, a remote host, the production connection string and a missing prerequisite are refused before any privileged connection. The assertions are per entry, per document and per business. The CI runs the suite against a PostgreSQL 18 service. |
| QB-33 | Payment truth: the settlement, the customer credit and the payment clearing | **DONE (the settlement truth)** | Only an acknowledged cash receipt settles a tender. A customer-credit sale creates a receivable and never a confirmed payment; a cheque or a transfer stays unsettled until an operator confirms it and is not bank money in the meantime. Verified against the real PostgreSQL. The open-item subledger itself is still to be built. |
| QB-35 | The goods receipt recognition in the ledger | **DONE (the receipt posts a balanced entry)** | The receipt posts DR inventory / CR goods received not invoiced in the same transaction as the movement; a return reverses it; the chart of accounts is verified before the posting. The critical finding F16 (the quantity moved while the ledger did not) is closed. The supplier bill that clears the account is still to be built. |
| QB-36 | The current business and location authority on every sensitive operation | **DONE (the current membership decides)** | The permission handler loads the active user, the lockout state and the active membership of the claimed business, and recomputes the permission from the current role. A revoked membership loses its authority immediately. The location scope and the granular permissions are still to be built. |
| QB-37 | The race-safe stock balance and the negative-stock policy | **DONE (one balance row, no negative stock)** | The balance row is created with a scoped upsert and then locked, so concurrent movements serialize on it; the deferred guard reads the current persisted state; the AllowNegative escape hatch is removed and negative stock is prohibited everywhere. The findings F17 and F18 are closed. |
| QB-38 | The perpetual stock valuation and the cost of goods sold | **DONE (the movement value is the authority)** | Every movement carries an authoritative value, the balance carries the value and the moving average, the cost of goods sold is the value the movement consumed (never the catalog price), the final depletion takes the exact remaining value and unvalued stock cannot be consumed. The critical finding F15 is closed. The historical stock still needs a finance-approved opening valuation. |
| QB-39 | The fail-closed, environment-bound cutover admission | **DONE (two modes, constant-time codeword, one environment)** | The read-only admission and the approved write are separate modes; the codeword comes from the environment and is compared in constant time; the database, the backup, the schema, the CLI and the HTTP target must name the same database; a skipped import is a failed gate; the script never enrols a device. The findings F01, F02 and F05 are closed. |
| QB-40 | The composite tenant foreign keys | **IN PROGRESS (waves 1-4 of 6 applied and proven)** | 35 single-column business-owned keys were read from the database; the existing data is consistent (0 cross-tenant rows), so the conversion is a constraint change and never a repair. The waves are ordered so that a parent and its dependants change together. |

## Current architecture state (after QB-07 / QB-08 / QB-09 / QB-10 / QB-11 / QB-12 / QB-13 / QB-14 / QB-16 / QB-17 / QB-18 / QB-19 / QB-20 / QB-21 / QB-22)

- `QtsModels` ™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ASP.NET Core host on **PostgreSQL**: Blazor app + `/api/v1` (auth, customers, products, accounts, periods, journal entries, trial balance, locations, inventory movements/balances/reconciliation, POS sales, cash registers/receipts/refunds, invoices/credit notes, suppliers, purchase orders with receipts/returns, expenses, tax rates, discounts, reports (balance sheet, profit and loss, cash, stock, sales summaries), audit events, health), cookie sessions with permission policies, typed ProblemDetails/correlation ids, contract JSON options, OpenAPI in Development.
- **Sales (QB-09).** One request posts the sale, line snapshots, stock movements, payment, balanced journal and audit event atomically; `Idempotency-Key` + payload fingerprint receipts make retries safe (replay returns the stored sale; a changed payload is refused).
- **Cash drawers, receipts and refunds (QB-10).** Cash sales can name a register with an open drawer session; movements are audited with reasons; expected cash is derived from movements and close stores counted/variance. Card capture is disabled until a verified PSP exists. Receipts render from snapshots (JSON + printable HTML, provisional template ready for offline). Refunds post the balanced reversal, return stock and record the drawer movement, replay-safe via `Idempotency-Key`.
- **Invoices and credits (QB-11).** Drafts are editable with server-computed totals; issuing allocates `INV-<year>-#####` inside the posting transaction and freezes snapshot lines. Payments allocate against the open balance with idempotency receipts; posted invoices are never edited - void (unpaid) or a full credit note that references the original and reverses its entry. Printable HTML invoice with DRAFT/VOID banners.
- **Purchasing, expenses and pricing (QB-12).** Purchase orders are commitments only; stock moves through idempotent receipts (`GRN-`) and returns (`PR-`) with quantity/cost snapshots validated under a row lock. Expenses post one balanced entry against cash/bank and reverse exactly once; tax rates are bounded exact decimals with a single default; discount definitions are validated (percentage 0-100, fixed non-negative, ordered window) before storage.
- **Reports from authoritative facts (QB-13).** Balance sheet, P&L, cash, stock and sales statements are computed per request from posted journal lines and inventory movements: no stored balances, base-currency-only, business-scoped responses (echoed ids), provisional offline money excluded by construction, and live tie-outs (sheet balanced, P&L = current earnings, cash = trial balance, stock = inventory balances).
- **Security, audit and recovery (QB-14).** API failures are typed ProblemDetails with stable codes and a correlation id (domain rules, 400/401/403/500; no stack/SQL leakage) and there is no database-writing error logger. Authentication outcomes are audited append-only with masked e-mails and request correlation, readable through the tenant-scoped `audit-events` feed (`audit.view`, Owner). Reset tickets are never logged; Data Protection keys can persist via `QTS_DATAPROTECTION_PATH`. Real `pg_dump` backups carry SHA-256 manifests, restore into a disposable database and reconcile (scripts + drill); the app role is DML-only and the static secret scan is clean.

shared MudBlazor Razor Class Library (customer page + dialog, layout, nav) used by the web client and by the future MAUI host. It references only `QtsBilling.Contracts` + MudBlazor, consumes `ICustomerApiClient`/`IAccountApiClient`, declares no render modes and owns no assets; hosts register the RCL assembly for SSR/client routing and load MudBlazor + shared scoped CSS. device-local store (QB-17): one WAL SQLite file per device with a discardable cache group and a durable pending group (provisional sales, cash movements, outbox, inbox receipts, device profile, sync cursors); money/quantity are checked scaled `INTEGER` values, writes are serialized by one gate, startup blocks on a failed integrity check or an unknown/newer schema and snapshots the file before ™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â Windows desktop client (QB-18/QB-19): .NET 10 MAUI Blazor Hybrid host for `net10.0-windows10.0.19041.0`. It renders the shared `QtsBilling.Ui` components in a WebView2, owns the host assets, the device identity (GUID v7), the DPAPI-backed session and the offline-tolerant API adapters, opens the device-local SQLite store at startup and writes a secret-free startup report plus an API-call log. The shared point-of-sale view captures cash sales into the device store through `ILocalPosService` (the browser host registers a stub that refuses offline capture); the money rules live next to the store, the receipt is explicitly provisional and the pending queue survives restarts.
- `QtsWebApp/Features/Sync` ™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â the idempotent sync-push handler (QB-20): `POST /api/v1/sync/operations` accepts a captured operation exactly once. The receipt row in `processed_operations` is written in the same transaction as the sale (the QB-09 posting path, extracted into `Features/Sales/SalePosting` and shared with the live POS endpoint), a replay returns the original outcome, a divergent body is a stable conflict, and the device, business and payload version are checked before anything is written.
- `QtsWebApp/Features/Sync` ™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â the master-data change feed (QB-21): `GET /api/v1/sync/changes` returns the commit-ordered changes of a business. The feed row is written in the same transaction as the master-data write, the per-business revision is allocated by a row-locked increment, a deactivation is a tombstone, the projections are permission scoped, and a cursor past the server is answered with `RESYNC_REQUIRED`.
- Cash register, invoices, purchasing, expenses and reporting all run on PostgreSQL is an out-of-band effort importing historical data from an external source. No dual writes exist; the PostgreSQL path serves its own surface.

## Local development environment (this machine)

```text
PostgreSQL 18.6 container: docker run -d --name qtsbilling-pg -p 5433:5432 \
  -e POSTGRES_USER=qtsbilling -e POSTGRES_PASSWORD=qtsbilling -e POSTGRES_DB=qtsbilling postgres:18.6
connection string:  QTS_POSTGRES_CONNECTION="Host=localhost;Port=5433;Database=qtsbilling;Username=qtsbilling;Password=qtsbilling"
run the web host:   dotnet run --project QtsWebApp.csproj --no-launch-profile --urls http://localhost:5188   (workdir QtsWebApp\QtsWebApp)
migrations:         dotnet ef database update --project QtsBilling.Persistence.Postgres   (from the repository root)
```

Development bootstrap: the first start applies migrations and creates one **Owner** account
(`owner@qtsbilling.local`, password from `QtsBilling:DevOwner:Password`, default `QtsBilling!Dev2026`)
whose setup is deliberately pending. Sign-in is refused with `PASSWORD_RESET_REQUIRED` until the
setup ticket (logged at startup, or returned by `POST /api/v1/auth/password-reset/request` in
Development) is completed via `POST /api/v1/auth/password-reset/complete`.

## Test status
